## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-16T04:26:10.351Z

```
你在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game（Terraria 1.4.5.6 复刻种子等价工程终局阶段）。任务：**全链最高价值狩猎**——jungle pass(#17) 的掷骰数分叉（原版真值链证实的唯一根因级分歧；terrain→grass 前 17 pass 已逐位复刻原版）。

## 现场（原版链注入代理已精确钉住）
- JS/oracle 在 jungle 检查点互相**绿**（同构），但两侧与**原版**掷骰数分叉 + 35.8 万格差——即 jungle 转录有一个**共同错**（两侧同错所以互检看不见）。
- 每 pass 重播已落地（jungle 从 stream(seed) 流 0 开始，与原版 RunPass 语义同构）——分歧不在流起点，在 pass 内部的某处掷骰消耗差异。
- **原版逐列真值在手**：/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g/ 目录有原版各 pass 末网格 dump（含 jungle 前后）；/tmp/sw-num4/passchain_x86.txt 是 9293480 的权威 106-pass 哈希链（每 pass 含 RNG 流指纹=掷骰数）。
- 对照工具现成：/tmp/sw-num4/cmpall.py（哈希对照）、diffcount_x86.py（逐检查点 diff 计数）。⚠️ 一切原版对照以 arch -x86_64 跑的为准。
- 另两代理在改 caves-oracle.cs 的 livingtrees 段和 dungeon lights 段——你只动 **jungle 段**（JunglePass.ts + oracle 的 jungle 部分），每次编辑前重读。

## 嫌疑清单（历史 jungle 改动全部在此，逐个对原版核）
1. `placeGemsAt` 偏移区间（我曾修开区间→闭区间——修的方向对不对用原版轨迹终判）；
2. 收尾 loop 宝石掷序（type 先于实参——C# 求值序再核）；
3. 分支洞穴 mudWall=true 的掷骰（我镜像的——JS 和 oracle 是否都多/少掷了）；
4. 巨型泥 runner 的 num3 液体类型掷（每次调用必掷）；
5. `generateTunnelToSurface` 的开门条件/`generateHolesInMudWalls` 拒绝采样；
6. flag4 quirk/侧裂隙 vy2/椭圆上界（2b 代理修过的所有点——都可能是"共同错"）。

## 方法
1. 用 oracle 跑 9293480 带 STREAM_ALL（/tmp/sw-num4/oracle-all.cs 有带流指纹的副本可参考改法）拿我们 jungle 的流指纹/掷骰数 vs 原版 passchain 的 jungle 行——差多少掷。
2. 在 JS/oracle 的 jungle 段逐阶段加掷骰计数（placeFirstPassMud/gems/巨型/tunnel/holes/finishing 六段各计数）vs 原版——哪个段差。原版侧：给沙盒探针加段级计数（复用 /tmp/sw-num4/patch 的 Cecil 模式）或用网格 diff 定位段。
3. 定位到具体掷点后对照 1456 JunglePass.cs 裁决修复（JS+oracle 同修）。
4. 金标再生（错峰：先查无 dotnet 在跑）+caves-checkpoint 双种子绿+**用 cmpall.py 复验 jungle 四哈希 vs 原版=逐位相等**（这是终局标准）。
5. 顺手复验 mudcaves 及下游 pass 是否大幅转绿（jungle 收口后原版侧级联应消）。

## 纪律
- 单次<8min；心跳轮询；原版沙盒跑批一律 arch -x86_64+HOME 隔离+超时+进程归零；exe 改前备份改后恢复。
- 报告：根因（1456 行号）+掷骰差异数+修复+原版对照前后哈希+下游复绿范围。
```

```
132	static TypeDefinition ImportProbeType(ModuleDefinition mod, string probeDll, string ns, string name)
133	{
134	    using var probeAsm = AssemblyDefinition.ReadAssembly(probeDll);
135	    var probeMod = probeAsm.MainModule;
136	    var srcType = probeMod.GetType(ns, name)
137	        ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(name + " not found in ProbeLib");
138	    var dstType = new TypeDefinition(srcType.Namespace, srcType.Name, srcType.Attributes, mod.TypeSystem.Object);
139	    foreach (var f in srcType.Fields)
140	        dstType.Fields.Add(new FieldDefinition(f.Name, f.Attributes, mod.ImportReference(f.FieldType)));
141	    foreach (var m in srcType.Methods)
142	    {
143	        var dm = new MethodDefinition(m.Name, m.Attributes, mod.ImportReference(m.ReturnType));
144	        foreach (var p in m.Parameters)
145	            dm.Parameters.Add(new ParameterDefinition(p.Name, p.Attributes, mod.ImportReference(p.ParameterType)));
146	        dstType.Methods.Add(dm);
147	    }
148	    foreach (var m in srcType.Methods)
149	        CopyBody(m, dstType.Methods.First(x => x.Name == m.Name), mod, srcType, dstType);
150	    mod.Types.Add(dstType);
151	    return dstType;
152	}
153	
154	static void Patch(ModuleDefinition mod, MethodDefinition applyPass, string probeDll)
155	{
156	    var dstType = ImportProbeType(mod, probeDll, "Terraria.GameContent.Biomes", "TerrainProbe");
157	    var enterRef = dstType.Methods.First(m => m.Name == "Enter");
158	    var colRef = dstType.Methods.First(m => m.Name == "Col");
159	
160	    var ip = applyPass.Body.GetILProcessor();
161	    var first = applyPass.Body.Instructions[0];
162	    ip.InsertBefore(first, ip.Create(OpCodes.Call, enterRef));
163	    Console.WriteLine("[inject] Enter() at ApplyPass entry");
164	
165	    var body = applyPass.Body;
166	    var injected = 0;
167	    for (int idx = 0; idx < body.Instructions.Count; idx++)
168	    {
169	        var cur = body.Instructions[idx];
170	        if (cur.OpCode.Code != Code.Call || cur.Operand is not MethodReference mr || mr.Name != "FillColumn") continue;
171	        static bool IsLdloc(OpCode op) => op.Code is Code.Ldloc or Code.Ldloc_0 or Code.Ldloc_1 or Code.Ldloc_2 or Code.Ldloc_3 or Code.Ldloc_S;
172	        var push = new List<Instruction>();
173	        for (int k = idx - 1; k >= 0 && push.Count < 3 && IsLdloc(body.Instructions[k].OpCode); k--)
174	            push.Add(body.Instructions[k]);
175	        push.Reverse();
176	        if (push.Count != 3) { Console.WriteLine($"[skip] IL_{cur.Offset:X4}: 非 [ldloc x3] 形态（push={push.Count}）"); continue; }
177	        var slots = push.Select(p => p.Slot()).ToArray();
178	        Console.WriteLine($"[inject] IL_{cur.Offset:X4} <- Col(V{slots[0]}, V{slots[1]}, V{slots[2]})");
179	        ip.InsertBefore(cur, ip.Create(OpCodes.Ldloc, body.Variables[slots[0]]));
180	        ip.InsertBefore(cur, ip.Create(OpCodes.Ldloc, body.Variables[slots[1]]));
181	        ip.InsertBefore(cur, ip.Create(OpCodes.Ldloc, body.Variables[slots[2]]));
182	        ip.InsertBefore(cur, ip.Create(OpCodes.Call, colRef));
183	        injected++;
184	        idx += 4;
185	    }
186	    if (injected == 0) throw new InvalidOperationException("no FillColumn call site injected");
187	}
188	
189	// 跨模块拷贝方法体：操作数令牌全部经 ImportReference 重定；同类型内成员指向克隆后的目标
190	static void CopyBody(MethodDefinition src, MethodDefinition dst, ModuleDefinition target, TypeDefinition srcType, TypeDefinition dstType)
191	{
192	    var map = new Dictionary<Instruction, Instruction>();
193	    dst.Body.InitLocals = src.Body.InitLocals;
194	    foreach (var v in src.Body.Variables)
195	        dst.Body.Variables.Add(new VariableDefinition(target.ImportReference(v.VariableType)));
196	    foreach (var ins in src.Body.Instructions)
197	    {
198	        Instruction ni;
199	        switch (ins.Operand)
200	        {
201	            case Instruction: ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, Instruction.Create(OpCodes.Nop)); break;
202	            case Instruction[]: ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, new[] { Instruction.Create(OpCodes.Nop) }); break;
203	            case MethodReference m:
204	                ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, m.DeclaringType == srcType
205	                    ? dstType.Methods.First(x => x.Name == m.Name && x.Parameters.Count == m.Parameters.Count)
206	                    : target.ImportReference(m));
207	                break;
208	            case FieldReference f:
209	                ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, f.DeclaringType == srcType
210	                    ? dstType.Fields.First(x => x.Name == f.Name)
211	                    : target.ImportReference(f));
212	                break;
213	            case TypeReference t: ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, target.ImportReference(t)); break;
214	            case VariableReference v: ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, dst.Body.Variables[v.Index]); break;
215	            case ParameterReference p: ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, dst.Parameters[p.Index]); break;
216	            default:
217	                if (ins.Operand == null) ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode);
218	                else if (ins.Operand is string s) ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, s);
219	                else if (ins.Operand is int n4) ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, n4);
220	                else if (ins.Operand is sbyte sb) ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, sb);
221	                else if (ins.Operand is long l4) ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, l4);
222	                else if (ins.Operand is double d4) ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, d4);
223	                else if (ins.Operand is float f4) ni = Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, f4);
224	                else throw new NotSupportedException($"operand {ins.Operand.GetType().Name} for {ins.OpCode}");
225	                break;
226	        }
227	        map[ins] = ni;
228	        dst.Body.Instructions.Add(ni);
229	    }
230	    var srcList = src.Body.Instructions;
231	    for (int k = 0; k < srcList.Count; k++)
232	    {
233	        var ni = dst.Body.Instructions[k];
234	        switch (srcList[k].Operand)
235	        {
236	            case Instruction t: ni.Operand = map[t]; break;
237	            case Instruction[] arr: ni.Operand = arr.Select(a => map[a]).ToArray(); break;
238	        }
239	    }
240	    foreach (var eh in src.Body.ExceptionHandlers)
241	        dst.Body.ExceptionHandlers.Add(new ExceptionHandler(eh.HandlerType)
242	        {
243	            TryStart = map[eh.TryStart],
244	            TryEnd = eh.TryEnd != null ? map[eh.TryEnd] : null,
245	            FilterStart = eh.FilterStart != null ? map[eh.FilterStart] : null,
246	            HandlerStart = eh.HandlerStart != null ? map[eh.HandlerStart] : null,
247	            HandlerEnd = eh.HandlerEnd != null ? map[eh.HandlerEnd] : null,
248	            CatchType = eh.CatchType != null ? target.ImportReference(eh.CatchType) : null
249	        });
250	}
251	
252	static class Ext
253	{
254	    public static int Slot(this Instruction ldloc) => ldloc.OpCode.Code switch
255	    {
256	        Code.Ldloc_0 => 0, Code.Ldloc_1 => 1, Code.Ldloc_2 => 2, Code.Ldloc_3 => 3,
257	        Code.Ldloc_S or Code.Ldloc => ((VariableReference)ldloc.Operand!).Index,
258	        _ => -1
259	    };
260	}
261
```

```
// 每 pass 末全网格 FNV 四哈希探针（终极真值链）。
// 挂 WorldGenerator.RunPass 尾（GenerationProgress.End() 之后、GenPassResult 构造前，
//   即 RandNext 抽骰之前——Main.rand 态 = 纯 ApplyPass 末态）。
// 算法与 tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs 的 Checkpoint()/StreamHash() 逐字节一致：
//   typ: act!=0 ? type : 0 → Fnv16（低字节先、一次 xor+mul 每字节）
//   act: Fnv8（每字节 xor+mul 再补一次纯 mul）
//   wal: wall → Fnv16；liq: liquid → Fnv8
//   gho: act==0 ? type : 0 → Fnv16（幽灵 parity 侧车）
//   stream: Main.rand(=WorldGen.genRand) SeedArray[56]+inext 的 FNV（同 oracle StreamHash）
// 遍历序 = oracle 的行主序 i = y*W + x（y 外层 x 内层）。
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text;

namespace Terraria.WorldBuilding
{
    public static class PassChainProbe
    {
        private static string _path;
        private static bool _header;
        private static int _idx;
        private static FieldInfo _fiSeedArray;
        private static FieldInfo _fiInext;
        private static long _totalMs;

        public static void EvilSetup()
        {
            try
            {
                string e = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_EVIL");
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e))
                {
                    int v = int.Parse(e);
                    Terraria.WorldGen.WorldGenParam_Evil = v;
                    File.AppendAllText(DumpPath(), "#evil-forced " + v + "\n");
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex) { Mark("#evil-ex " + ex.GetType().Name + ": " + ex.Message); }
        }

        private static string DumpPath()
        {
            if (_path == null)
            {
                _path = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_PASSCHAIN");
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_path)) _path = "/tmp/sw-num4/home/passchain_default.txt";
            }
            return _path;
        }

        public static void Tail(GenPass pass)
        {
            try
            {
                Stopwatch sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                int W = Terraria.Main.maxTilesX, H = Terraria.Main.maxTilesY;
                Terraria.Tile[,] tile = Terraria.Main.tile;
                uint hT = 2166136261u, hA = 2166136261u, hW = 2166136261u, hL = 2166136261u, hG = 2166136261u;
                int n = 0;
                for (int y = 0; y < H; y++)
                {
                    int rowBase = y * W;
                    for (int x = 0; x < W; x++)
                    {
                        Terraria.Tile t = tile[x, y];
                        bool act = t.active();
                        ushort typ = act ? t.type : (ushort)0;
                        uint lo = (uint)(typ & 0xFF);
                        uint hi = (uint)(typ >> 8);
                        hT = (hT ^ lo) * 16777619u;
                        hT = (hT ^ hi) * 16777619u;
                        byte a = act ? (byte)1 : (byte)0;
                        hA = (hA ^ a) * 16777619u;
                        hA = hA * 16777619u;
                        ushort wv = t.wall;
                        lo = (uint)(wv & 0xFF);
                        hi = (uint)(wv >> 8);
                        hW = (hW ^ lo) * 16777619u;
                        hW = (hW ^ hi) * 16777619u;
                        byte l = t.liquid;
                        hL = (hL ^ l) * 16777619u;
                        hL = hL * 16777619u;
                        ushort gho = act ? (ushort)0 : t.type;
                        lo = (uint)(gho & 0xFF);
                        hi = (uint)(gho >> 8);
                        hG = (hG ^ lo) * 16777619u;
                        hG = (hG ^ hi) * 16777619u;
                        n++;
                    }
                }
                sw.Stop();
                _totalMs += sw.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                DumpGrids(pass.Name, W, H, tile);
                string line = "P|" + (_idx++) + "|" + pass.Name + "|" +
                    Hex(hT) + " " + Hex(hA) + " " + Hex(hW) + " " + Hex(hL) + "|" +
                    Hex(hG) + "|" + StreamHash() + "|" +
                    Terraria.WorldGen.WorldGenParam_Evil + "," + (Terraria.WorldGen.crimson ? 1 : 0) + "|" +
                    sw.ElapsedMilliseconds + "ms/" + _totalMs + "ms|n=" + n;
                if (!_header)
                {
                    _header = true;
                    string head = "#passchain";
                    try
                    {
                        head += " seed=" + Terraria.Main.ActiveWorldFileData.Seed +
                                " seedText=" + Terraria.Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText +
                                " W=" + Terraria.Main.maxTilesX + " H=" + Terraria.Main.maxTilesY;
                    }
                    catch { }
                    File.AppendAllText(DumpPath(), head + "\n");
                }
                File.AppendAllText(DumpPath(), line + "\n");
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                Mark("#tail-ex " + e.GetType().Name + ": " + e.Message + "\n" + e.StackTrace);
            }
        }

        private static string Hex(uint v)
        {
            return v.ToString("x8");
        }

        // SW_GRID_DUMP="PassName:/pfx[,PassName2:/pfx2]"：pass 名命中时落
        // typ(LE16)/act/wal(LE16)/liq 裸数组（i=y*W+x 序，与 caves-oracle GRID_DUMP 同格式；
        // typ 为 RAW——保留 inactive 格残留 type（幽灵），与 oracle 一致）
        // SW_DUMP_ALL=<dir>：每个 pass 都落 <dir>/<idx>_<名>.{typ,act,wal,liq}
        private static void DumpGrids(string passName, int W, int H, Terraria.Tile[,] tile)
        {
            string dumpAll = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_DUMP_ALL");
            if (dumpAll != null)
            {
                try
                {
                    StringBuilder sbn = new StringBuilder();
                    foreach (char ch in passName)
                        sbn.Append(char.IsLetterOrDigit(ch) ? ch : '_');
                    WriteGrid(dumpAll + "/" + _idx.ToString("000") + "_" + sbn.ToString(), W, H, tile);
                }
                catch (Exception e) { Mark("#dumpall-ex " + passName + " " + e.GetType().Name + ": " + e.Message); }
                return;
            }
            string cfg = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_GRID_DUMP");
            if (cfg == null) return;
            foreach (string one in cfg.Split(','))
            {
                string[] kv = one.Split(':');
                if (kv.Length != 2 || kv[0] != passName) continue;
                try
                {
                    WriteGrid(kv[1], W, H, tile);
                    Mark("#griddump " + passName + " -> " + kv[1]);
                }
                catch (Exception e) { Mark("#griddump-ex " + passName + " " + e.GetType().Name + ": " + e.Message); }
            }
        }

        private static void WriteGrid(string p, int W, int H, Terraria.Tile[,] tile)
        {
            int n = W * H;
            byte[] typ = new byte[n * 2];
            byte[] act = new byte[n];
            byte[] wal = new byte[n * 2];
            byte[] liq = new byte[n];
            int i = 0;
            for (int y = 0; y < H; y++)
                for (int x = 0; x < W; x++, i++)
                {
                    Terraria.Tile t = tile[x, y];
                    ushort ty2 = t.type;
                    typ[i * 2] = (byte)(ty2 & 0xFF);
                    typ[i * 2 + 1] = (byte)(ty2 >> 8);
                    act[i] = t.active() ? (byte)1 : (byte)0;
                    ushort wv = t.wall;
                    wal[i * 2] = (byte)(wv & 0xFF);
                    wal[i * 2 + 1] = (byte)(wv >> 8);
                    liq[i] = t.liquid;
                }
            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".typ", typ);
            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".act", act);
            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".wal", wal);
            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".liq", liq);
        }

        private static string StreamHash()
        {
            try
            {
                object r = Terraria.Main.rand;
                if (r == null) return "norand";
                if (_fiSeedArray == null)
                {
                    Type t = r.GetType();
                    _fiSeedArray = t.GetField("SeedArray", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
                    _fiInext = t.GetField("inext", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
                }
                int[] sa = (int[])_fiSeedArray.GetValue(r);
                uint ix = (uint)_fiInext.GetValue(r);
                uint h = 2166136261u;
                for (int k = 0; k < sa.Length; k++)
                {
                    int v = sa[k];
                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 8) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 16) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 24) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                }
                h = (h ^ (ix & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                h = (h ^ (uint)((ix >> 8) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                h = (h ^ (uint)((ix >> 16) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                h = (h ^ (uint)((ix >> 24) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
                return Hex(h);
            }
            catch (Exception e) { return "ex:" + e.GetType().Name; }
        }

        private static void Mark(string s)
        {
            try { File.AppendAllText("/tmp/sw-num4/home/passchain_err.txt", s + "\n"); } catch { }
        }
    }
}

```

```
Now I understand the passchain format:
`P|idx|Name|hT hA hW hL|hG|streamHash|evil,crimson|time|n`

- hT = typ hash (act? type:0)
- hA = act hash
- hW = wall hash
- hL = liquid hash
- hG = ghost hash (act==0 ? type : 0)
- streamHash = FNV of Main.rand SeedArray[56] + inext — this is the "RNG 流指纹" (stream fingerprint). It's not directly a roll count, but a state fingerprint. 137					if (value2 < 5)
```
```
138					{
139						value2 = 5;
140					}
141					if (Main.tile[value, value2].wall == 0 && !Main.tile[value, value2].active() && Main.tile[value, value2 - 3].wall == 0 && !Main.tile[value, value2 - 3].active() && Main.tile[value, value2 - 1].wall == 0 && !Main.tile[value, value2 - 1].active() && Main.tile[value, value2 - 4].wall == 0 && !Main.tile[value, value2 - 4].active() && Main.tile[value, value2 - 2].wall == 0 && !Main.tile[value, value2 - 2].active() && Main.tile[value, value2 - 5].wall == 0 && !Main.tile[value, value2 - 5].active())
142					{
143						flag = false;
144					}
145				}
146				GenVars.JungleX = (int)val.X;
147				num += (double)GenBase._random.Next(-20, 21) * 0.1;
148				if (num < 5.0)
149				{
150					num = 5.0;
151				}
152				if (num > 10.0)
153				{
154					num = 10.0;
155				}
156				int value3 = (int)(val.X - num * 0.5);
157				int value4 = (int)(val.X + num * 0.5);
158				int value5 = (int)(val.Y - num * 0.5);
159				int value6 = (int)(val.Y + num * 0.5);
160				int num3 = Utils.Clamp(value3, 10, Main.maxTilesX - 10);
161				value4 = Utils.Clamp(value4, 10, Main.maxTilesX - 10);
162				value5 = Utils.Clamp(value5, 10, Main.maxTilesY - 10);
163				value6 = Utils.Clamp(value6, 10, Main.maxTilesY - 10);
164				for (int k = num3; k < value4; k++)
165				{
166					for (int l = value5; l < value6; l++)
167					{
168						if (Math.Abs((double)k - val.X) + Math.Abs((double)l - val.Y) < num * 0.5 * (1.0 + (double)GenBase._random.Next(-10, 11) * 0.015))
169						{
170							WorldGen.KillTile(k, l);
171						}
172					}
173				}
174				num2++;
175				if (num2 > 10 && GenBase._random.Next(50) < num2)
176				{
177					num2 = 0;
178					int num4 = -2;
179					if (GenBase._random.Next(2) == 0)
180					{
181						num4 = 2;
182					}
183					WorldGen.TileRunner((int)val.X, (int)val.Y, GenBase._random.Next(3, 20), GenBase._random.Next(10, 100), -1, addTile: false, num4);
184				}
185				val += val2;
186				val2.Y += (double)GenBase._random.Next(-10, 11) * 0.01;
187				if (val2.Y > 0.0)
188				{
189					val2.Y = 0.0;
190				}
191				if (val2.Y < -2.0)
192				{
193					val2.Y = -2.0;
194				}
195				val2.X += (double)GenBase._random.Next(-10, 11) * 0.1;
196				if (val.X < (double)(i - 200))
197				{
198					val2.X += (double)GenBase._random.Next(5, 21) * 0.1;
199				}
200				if (val.X > (double)(i + 200))
201				{
202					val2.X -= (double)GenBase._random.Next(5, 21) * 0.1;
203				}
204				if (val2.X > 1.5)
205				{
206					val2.X = 1.5;
207				}
208				if (val2.X < -1.5)
209				{
210					val2.X = -1.5;
211				}
212			}
213		}
```

```
14	export function runJunglePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): number {

23	    x += rng.int(Math.floor(-xRange * scale), Math.floor(xRange * scale));
24	    y += rng.int(Math.floor(-yRange * scale), Math.floor(yRange * scale));
25	    y = Math.max(gs.rockLevel, Math.min(h, y)); // 原版 clamp 上界 = maxTilesY（H 非 H-1）
26	  };
27	  const placeFirstPassMud = (xSpeedScale: number) => {
28	    // 原版 Next((int)(250*ws), (int)(500*ws)) 上界开 → int(a, b-1)（曾多 1 档值）
29	    const str = rng.int(Math.floor(250 * scale), Math.floor(500 * scale) - 1);
30	    // 原版步数固定 50-150 不缩放(speedX=±3 → 漂移 ≤450;4200 宽的 10.7%)
31	    const stp = rng.int(50, 149);
32	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: str, steps: stp, type: MUD, speedX: gs.dungeonSide * xSpeedScale, speedY: 0, mudWall: true });
33	  };
34	  const placeGemsAt = (baseGem: number, variants: number) => {
35	    // 原版 Next(-(int)(125*ws), (int)(125*ws)) 上界开 → int(a, b-1)。
36	    // 曾写成闭区间多 1 档：偏移 187 时 gem runner 落点差 1 → 巨型泥 runner
37	    // 扫描内出现 1 格骰偏移（值不同但总数自愈）→ 丛林 ~292 格写出差异。
38	    for (let n = 0; n < 6 * scale; n++) {
39	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs,
40	        x + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale) - 1),
41	        y + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale) - 1), {
42	          strength: rng.int(3, 6), steps: rng.int(3, 7),
43	          type: GEMS[baseGem + rng.int(0, variants - 1)],
44	        });
45	    }
46	  };
47	
48	  applyRandomMovement(100, 100);
49	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
50	  placeFirstPassMud(3);

52	  applyRandomMovement(250, 150);

64	  // 原版 Next((int)(400*ws), (int)(600*ws)) 上界开（曾闭区间多 1 档）
65	  const num2 = rng.int(Math.floor(400 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
66	  const num3 = Math.floor(25 * scale);

68	  // JunglePass.cs:50-54：noYChange:true——vy=-20 保持原速直上；缺失时被钳到 ±1
69	  // （曾致泥柱矮、泥量大范围横向堆积，丛林地下形态与原版明显不同）
70	  tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i, cy, { strength: num2, steps: 10000, type: MUD, speedY: -20, noYChange: true, mudWall: true });
71	  // 泥柱中心记录给后续(丛林表面草化)
72	  (gs as any).jungleCoreX = i;
73	  const jungleXOut = generateTunnelToSurface(st, rng, gs, i, cy);
74	  generateHolesInMudWalls(st, rng, gs);

79	function generateTunnelToSurface(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number): number {
80	  let radius = rng.int(5, 10);
81	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
82	  let vx = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
83	  let vy = rng.int(10, 19) * 0.1;
84	  let count = 0;
85	  let running = true;
86	  let jungleX = i;
87	  while (running) {
88	    if (py < gs.worldSurface) {
89	      const cx = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px)));
90	      const cy = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py)));
91	      // 隧道顶到达无墙无块的开放区即停(检查上方 1-5 格)
92	      let open = true;
93	      for (let d = 0; d <= 5; d++) {
94	        const ii = st.idx(cx, Math.max(5, cy - d));
95	        if (st.wall[ii] !== 0 || st.flags[ii]) { open = false; break; }
96	      }
97	      if (open) running = false;
98	    }
99	    jungleX = Math.floor(px);
100	    radius += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1;
101	    if (radius < 5) radius = 5;
102	    if (radius > 10) radius = 10;
103	    const x0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px - radius * 0.5)));
104	    const x1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px + radius * 0.5)));
105	    const y0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py - radius * 0.5)));
106	    const y1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py + radius * 0.5)));
107	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
108	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
109	        if (Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py) < radius * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)) {
110	          // 原版 KillTile：只 active(false) **保留 type**（幽灵语义）——清零会
111	          // 让后续拒绝采样（查 type==59）判定分叉
112	          st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
113	        }
114	      }
115	    }
116	    count++;
117	    // 偶发分支洞穴
118	    if (count > 10 && rng.int(0, 49) < count) {
119	      count = 0;
120	      const side = rng.next() < 0.5 ? 2 : -2;   // 原版 num4 默认 -2、Next(2)==0 才 +2(JunglePass.cs:178-182),曾写反
121	      // ★mudWall=true(JunglePass.cs:54 置位、:59 才复位):隧道段整体在 mudWall 期,
122	      //   分支洞穴 TileRunner(type=-1)同样走 WorldGen.cs:77233 铺墙分支——每过
123	      //   `l>worldSurface && wall(k,l-1)!=2` 的格耗 1 颗 Next(3),过阈值再耗
124	      //   1 颗 Next(-10,11),铺丛林墙 15/64。漏传则隧道分支洞穴零掷骰零铺墙,
125	      //   流位整体前移(TileRunner.mudWall 分支已按原版实现,见 TileRunner.ts:119)
126	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(px), Math.floor(py), {
127	        strength: rng.int(3, 19), steps: rng.int(10, 99), type: -1, speedX: side, mudWall: true,
128	      });
129	    }
130	    px += vx; py += vy;
131	    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.01;
132	    if (vy > 0) vy = 0;
133	    if (vy < -2) vy = -2;
134	    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
135	    if (px < i - 200) vx += rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
136	    if (px > i + 200) vx -= rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
137	    if (vx > 1.5) vx = 1.5;
138	    if (vx < -1.5) vx = -1.5;
139	    if (py < -50 || py > st.h + 50) running = false; // 安全阀
140	  }
141	  return jungleX;
142	}

144	/** 泥墙挖洞（cs:12503-12520 + MudWallRunner cs:77584-77663 1:1）：
145	 *  w/4 次：拒绝采样到墙 64/15 格后跑 MudWallRunner（TileRunner 形状的清墙游走）。
146	 *  曾是"简化随机走"自创实现——掷骰序列完全不同 */
147	function generateHolesInMudWalls(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
148	  const { w, h } = st;
149	  const underworld = h - 200; // Main.UnderworldLayer（Main.cs:2863）
150	  const tries = Math.floor(w / 4);
151	  for (let n = 0; n < tries; n++) {
152	    let x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
153	    let y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, underworld - 1); // Next(wS+10, UnderworldLayer)
154	    while (!(st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 64 || st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 15)) {
155	      x = rng.int(20, w - 21);
156	      y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, underworld - 1);
157	    }
158	    mudWallRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y);
159	  }
160	}
161	
162	/** MudWallRunner（cs:77584）：衰减半径游走，菱形域内（y>worldSurface）清墙 */
163	function mudWallRunner(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number): void {
164	  const { w, h } = st;
165	  const num = rng.int(8, 20) + 0.0;          // Next(8,21)（double）
166	  const num2 = rng.int(8, 32) + 0.0;         // Next(8,33)（double）
167	  let num3 = num2;
168	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
169	  let vx = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1, vy = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
170	  while (num > 0 && num3 > 0) {
171	    const num4 = num * (num3 / num2);
172	    num3 -= 1;
173	    let x0 = Math.trunc(px - num4 * 0.5), x1 = Math.trunc(px + num4 * 0.5);
174	    let y0 = Math.trunc(py - num4 * 0.5), y1 = Math.trunc(py + num4 * 0.5);
175	    if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0;
176	    if (x1 > w) x1 = w;
177	    if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0;
178	    if (y1 > h) y1 = h;
179	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
180	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
181	        if (Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py) < num * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)
182	          && y > gs.worldSurface) {
183	          st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
184	        }
185	      }
186	    }
187	    px += vx; py += vy;
188	    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
189	    if (vx > 1) vx = 1;
190	    if (vx < -1) vx = -1;
191	    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
192	    if (vy > 1) vy = 1;
193	    if (vy < -1) vy = -1;
194	  }
195	}
196	
197	function generateFinishingTouches(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, oldX: number, oldY: number, scale: number, van: number): void {
198	  void van;
199	  let x = oldX, y = oldY;
200	  // 大泥簇
201	  for (let n = 0; n <= 20 * scale; n++) {
202	    // 原版 Next((int)(-5*ws), (int)(6*ws))：上界 6 非 5（曾写对称 ±5）
203	    x += rng.int(-Math.floor(5 * scale), Math.floor(6 * scale) - 1);
204	    y += rng.int(-Math.floor(5 * scale), Math.floor(6 * scale) - 1);

206	    const str = rng.int(40, 99), stp = rng.int(300, 499);

208	  }
209	  // 洞穴(1/7 填液体)
210	  for (let n = 0; n <= 10 * scale; n++) {
211	    // Next(-A, A) 上界开 → int(-A, A-1)；拒绝采样无界 while（原版，无 40 次上限）
212	    let i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
213	    let j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale) - 1);
214	    while (!(i2 >= 1 && i2 < st.w - 1 && j2 >= 1 && j2 < st.h - 1 && st.type[st.idx(i2, j2)] === MUD)) {
215	      i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale) - 1);
216	      j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale) - 1);
217	    }
218	    for (let k = 0; k < 8 * scale; k++) {
219	      i2 += rng.int(-30, 30);

221	      // ★原版掷序（cs:245-247）：type(Next 7) → strength(Next 10,20) → steps(Next 30,70)
```

```
227	/**
228	 * 原版 WorldGen.digTunnel(L48132)移植:蜿蜒洞穴原语。
229	 * 速度 = (dir + 噪声游走±0.2/步) × 0.6 → 会拐弯;半径在 [Size×0.6, Size×2] 随机波动 → 粗细变化。
230	 * 这是有机洞型的来源(TileRunner strength<50 时恒速恒宽 = 直线刀砍)。
231	 */
232	export function digTunnel(
233	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG,
234	  X: number, Y: number, xDir: number, yDir: number,
235	  steps: number, size: number, wet = false,
236	): [number, number] {
237	  let x = Math.max(size + 1, Math.min(st.w - size - 1, X));
238	  let y = Math.max(size + 1, Math.min(st.h - size - 1, Y));
239	  let noiseX = 0, noiseY = 0;
240	  let radius = size;
241	  for (let step = 0; step < steps; step++) {
242	    // (int) 向零截断（cs:79766-79767）：floor 在负小数时多扫一列 → 多耗 dist 骰
243	    const x0 = Math.trunc(x - radius), x1 = Math.floor(x + radius);
244	    const y0 = Math.trunc(y - radius), y1 = Math.floor(y + radius);
245	    for (let ix = x0; ix <= x1; ix++) {
246	      for (let iy = y0; iy <= y1; iy++) {
247	        if (Math.abs(ix - x) + Math.abs(iy - y) < radius * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.005)
248	          && st.inBounds(ix, iy)) {
249	          const i = st.idx(ix, iy);
250	          st.flags[i] = 0; // 原版 active(false) 保留 type（幽灵）——清零分叉拒绝采样
251	          if (wet) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
252	        }
253	      }
254	    }
255	    radius += rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.03;
256	    if (radius < size * 0.6) radius = size * 0.6;
257	    if (radius > size * 2) radius = size * 2;
258	    noiseX += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.01;
259	    noiseY += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.01;
260	    noiseX = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, noiseX));
261	    noiseY = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, noiseY));
262	    x += (xDir + noiseX) * 0.6;
263	    y += (yDir + noiseY) * 0.6;
264	  }
265	  return [x, y];
266	}
267	
268	function inUndergroundDesert(gs: GenState, x: number, y: number): boolean {
269	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
270	  return !!ud && x >= ud.x0 && x <= ud.x1 && y >= ud.y0 && y <= ud.y1;
271	}
272
```

````
public virtual int Next(int minValue, int maxValue) {

}
````

```
	private static void AttemptFossilShattering(int i, int j, Tile tileCache, bool fail)
	{

			return;
		}
		fossilBreak = true;
		for (int k = i - 1; k <= i + 1; k++)
		{
			for (int l = j - 1; l <= j + 1; l++)
			{
				int maxValue = 15;

					maxValue = 4;
				}
				else if (k == i && l == j - 1 && !fail)

					maxValue = 4;
				}
				if ((k != i || l != j) && Main.tile[k, l].active() && Main.tile[k, l].type == 404 && genRand.Next(maxValue) == 0)

					}
				}
			}
		}
```

```
#!/bin/bash
# MonoKickstart Shell Script
# Written by Ethan "flibitijibibo" Lee

cd "`dirname "$0"`"

UNAME=`uname`
ARCH=`uname -m`

if [ "$UNAME" == "Darwin" ]; then
	export DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH=./osx/:$DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH

	if [ "$STEAM_DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES" != "" ] && [ "$DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES" == "" ]; then
		export DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES="$STEAM_DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES"
	fi
	
	if [ "$SteamClientLaunch" = "1" ] && [ "$ARCH" = "x86_64" ] && [ "$(sysctl -n sysctl.proc_translated 2>/dev/null || echo 0)" = "1" ]; then
		arch -arm64 ./TerrariaServer.bin.osx $@
	else
		./TerrariaServer.bin.osx $@
	fi
fi
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-16T04:56:12.300Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
The JS edit looks good. Note: placeWall uses `st.wall` — but in the hot loop `twall` is the localized reference — same array, fine.

One catch: JS `rng.nextIntRange(0, 4)` at line 101 — this is Next(0,4). Vanilla is `genRand.Next(4)` (Next(maxValue)). Next(maxValue) = `(int)(Sample() * maxValue)`; Next(0,4) with range 4 ≤ 255 → `(int)(Sample() * 4) + 0`. BOTH consume exactly one Sample() and produce the same distribution... but are they bit-identical? .NET's Random.Next(maxValue): 
```
public virtual int Next(int maxValue) {
    if (maxValue<0) throw...
    return (int)(Sample()*maxValue);
}
```
And Terraria's UnifiedRandom overrides Next(int maxValue)? Let me check the Terraria UnifiedRandom source to be sure both consume one InternalSample identically. Since this existed BEFORE my change and the pre-jungle chain (which uses tons of TileRunner via caves passes with... wait — do pre-jungle passes use TileRunner? YES — dirt/rock layer caves use Caverer→TileRunner... and the checkpoints are GREEN vs vanilla. So nextIntRange(0,4) ≡ Next(4) has been empirically validated by the green chain. Good.

Now let me retry the sandbox command.
````

````
MASSIVE progress:
- j_r1 through j_tunnel now MATCH vanilla exactly (4c58f747, 3bbf5936, 811ec8ce, 9efaa402, a5321d34) ✓✓✓✓✓
- Divergence now starts at **j_holes** (oracle d2957d1d vs vanilla 722d69f4).

So the PlaceWall + KillTile fixes nailed the first five segments. The remaining divergence is in GenerateHolesInMudWalls (rejection sampling + MudWallRunner).

Note: the stream diverges during holes. What differs there?
- The rejection sampling reads WALLS: `while (wall != 64 && wall != 15)` — if the oracle's wall grid at this point differs from vanilla, resample counts differ. Since j_tunnel matches (same rolls to that point) and mud runners' wall placement semantics now match, the wall grid SHOULD match... unless the wall grid diverged earlier without affecting rolls (e.g., walls written by segments whose rolls match — possible if a wall-write difference doesn't affect subsequent rolls until holes).

What could differ in the wall grid despite identical rolls?
````

```
interface Golden {
  checkpoints: string[]; stream: string[];
  worldSurface: number; rockLayer: number;
  shimmerX: number; shimmerY: number; dungeonRight: number;
}
function loadGolden(file: string) {
  const golden = JSON.parse(
    readFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL(`./golden/${file}`, import.meta.url)), 'utf8'),
  ) as Golden;
  const gold = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const line of golden.checkpoints) {
    const [name, typ, act, , liq] = line.split(/[| ]+/);
    gold.set(name, `${typ} ${act} ${liq}`);
  }
  const goldStream = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const line of golden.stream) {
    const [name, hash] = line.split('|');
    goldStream.set(name, hash);
  }
  return { golden, gold, goldStream };
}
```

```
Summary of everything:

**Root causes (3 "common errors" — JS and oracle both wrong identically, hence mutually green but both diverging from vanilla):**

1. **PlaceWall semantics in TileRunner's mudWall branch** (WorldGen.cs:75189 + Framing.cs:388-395):
   - Vanilla PlaceWall only writes when `tile.wall == 0` (and bounds (1,W-2)×(1,H-2)) — our code overwrote unconditionally.
   - Every successful placement calls SquareWallFrame → Framing.WallFrame(resetFrame:true) which consumes **one `genRand.Next(0,3)`** (walls 15/64/178/180 are not in wallLargeFrames 1/2 tables). We consumed ZERO.
   - Impact: stream diverged from the very first mud runner (j_r1) — missing tens of thousands of rolls + wrong wall grid (281K wall diffs at jungle checkpoint).

2. **KillTile zeroes type** (WorldGen.cs:63970 `tile.type = 0` at tail; worldgen path: no sounds, no liquid special-casing on server; SquareTileFrame's Next(0,3) is behind `!generatingWorld` so NOT rolled):
   - The tunnel's `WorldGen.KillTile(k,l)` ellipse sets active=false AND type=0 for ACTIVE cells (inactive cells early-return unchanged — TileRunner's type<0 dig path keeps ghost type, that part was right).
   - Our "keep type" misreading polluted the typ grid and the finishing-touches rejection sampling (`type != 59` reads ghosts).

3. **GenerateHolesInMudWalls uses GenVars.worldSurface, not Main.worldSurface** (JunglePass.cs:220 `(int)GenVars.worldSurface + 10` vs TerrainPass.cs:206/235):
   - GenVars.worldSurface = TerrainPass's final walked `num3` (last column's surface), NOT Main.worldSurface (= max+25=337).
   - Both consume 1 roll per sample regardless of range → stream hashes stayed equal while sampled y values differed → wall clears shifted (grid diverged, hash-preserving) until iteration 12 where rejection counts actually diverged.
   - JS already had `gs.genWorldSurface`; oracle uses `num3w`.
```

```
狩猎完成。最终报告：

## 根因：jungle 掷骰分叉 = 三处「共同错」（JS 与 oracle 同错，互检盲区）

原版链注入探针（Cecil 补丁 `JunglePass.ApplyPass` 7 段边界 + holes 每轮流指纹，`/tmp/sw-num4/probelib/JungleProbe.cs`、`patch/Program.cs` jungle 模式，产物 `TerrariaServer.jungle.exe`）逐步钉死：

**① PlaceWall 语义（首个分歧点 j_r1，修复前最大源）** — `WorldGen.cs:75199` PlaceWall **仅在 `wall==0` 时落墙**（边界 (1,W-2)×(1,H-2)），且每次成功落墙经 `SquareWallFrame→Framing.WallFrame(resetFrame:true)` **掷 1 颗 `Next(0,3)`**（`Framing.cs:390`；墙 15/64/178/180 不在 wallLargeFrames 1/2 表 → 必掷，且无 generatingWorld 门）。我们曾无条件覆写+零掷骰 → 首个泥 runner 起流即偏（jungle 检查点 28.1 万墙差 + 每墙缺 1 掷的流级偏移）。

**② KillTile 清 type（WorldGen.cs:63970）** — 隧道椭圆 `KillTile(k,l)` 对活性格 `active=false` **且 `type=0`**（SquareTileFrame 的 Next(0,3) 在 `!generatingWorld` 门内不掷；非活性格早退保留幽灵）。曾误记"保留 type" → typ 网污染 + 收尾拒绝采样（查 `type!=59`）读到幽灵泥。

**③ holes 采样下界用 GenVars 非 Main（JunglePass.cs:220）** — `Next((int)GenVars.worldSurface+10, …)` 的 GenVars 值 = Terrain 游走**终值 num3**（TerrainPass.cs:235），≠ Main.worldSurface（=max+25=337）。每样本恒耗 1 掷 → **流哈希不变而 y 落点平移**（正是"掷骰数分叉但互检绿"的隐形形态）：墙网先错位、直到第 12 轮拒绝次数才真正分叉（j_h0-j_h11 哈希全同、j_h11 墙网已差 3557 格）。

## 修复（JS+oracle 双修）
- `game/src/world/gen/vanilla/TileRunner.ts`：新增 `placeWall()`（边界+wall==0 门+`rng.int(0,2)` 掷）；mudWall 分支与 granite/marble 伴生墙分支改走它。
- `game/src/world/gen/vanilla/JunglePass.ts`：隧道 KillTile 活性格清 `type=0`；holes 下界 `Math.trunc(gs.genWorldSurface)+10`。
- `game/tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs`：`PlaceWall` 局部函数（`r.Next(0,3)`）、隧道同修、`holeLo=(int)num3w+10`；JSeg 段级流指纹基建（.jseg 边车）保留复用。

## 原版对照（终局标准全过）
- 段流指纹：j_r1/j_r2/j_r3/j_big/j_tunnel/j_holes/j_end **七段全等** + 1050 轮 holes 全等；j_end=`a33b0dcb` == passchain_x86 权威值。
- 四哈希：jungle=`1dbe60fe 99721e54 a662ed58 d5716822`、mudcaves=`77702128 9b3d553c a662ed58 d5716822` == 原版 pass 17/18（含 ghost `0cf8763a`）。
- **逐位相等**：jungle 与 mudcaves 网格 typ/act/wal/liq 四面 0 差（4200×1200 全格）。

## 下游复绿与测试
```
